- **see-it-in-figma**: Prototype a design problem in Figma against Cornerstone's real variables and components, before anyone commits to an implementation. Figma only — it never writes application code. - **seed**: seed.md ([agenticnotetaking/eidos](https://github.com/agenticnotetaking/eidos/tree/HEAD/skills/seed) · ★59)
- **seed**: Read the current Claude Code session's raw activity log and help the user synthesize it into tweet material. Use when user says /seed, 'save this for twitter', 'this is tweet material', 'capture this', '记一下', '发推', or wants to turn recent work into a tweet. ([Yrzhe/claude-skills](https://github.com/Yrzhe/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/seed/skills/seed) · ★34)
- **seed**: Database seeding, test fixtures, factory patterns, fake data generation. Idempotent seed scripts. ([arbazkhan971/godmode](https://github.com/arbazkhan971/godmode/tree/HEAD/skills/seed) · ★26)
- **seed**: Generate FK-aware, deterministic sample/seed data from a database schema, ORM model, or migrations — INSERT statements emitted in dependency order (parents first), values derived from row index so output is stable and reviewable. Read-only; never writes to a database. For dev/test fixtures only. Use when the user asks to seed, generate sample/test/fixture/dummy/fake data, populate tables, or create starter rows for Postgres, MySQL, SQLite, and friends. ([Hainrixz/claude-db](https://github.com/Hainrixz/claude-db/tree/HEAD/skills/seed) · ★19)
- **seed**: Use immediately after gate 1 (stories approved) to settle which kernel edition the project is built on, create the project repo from it, and make the method durable inside it. Also use when an existing X2 project is missing its CLAUDE.md standing constraints or its turn ledger. Do not use before stories are approved, and never use it to modify the kernel itself. ([teklabsdigital/x2-method](https://github.com/teklabsdigital/x2-method/tree/HEAD/skills/seed) · ★15)
- **seed**: Build a portable starter slice from this learning-loop instance for a fresh instance to boot from. Usage: /learning-loop:seed [--for-job] [--types feedback,reference] [--out &lt;dir&gt;]. Default --for-job carries working-style (feedback memories + _system + portable prefs), no vault notes, no projects. Use when ramping up at a new job, a new project vault, or a second machine.
